This couples two design choices: the learning-rate schedule that generates the parameter trajectory and the estimator that constructs the deployed model (e.g. the raw final iterate or a checkpoint average). A schedule that promotes optimization progress may differ from one that minimizes variation in the raw final iterate. Separating these choices creates an opportunity to maintain progress late in training whil","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-21T23:19:17Z","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2609_25482.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作指出 LLM 预训练末端存在一个被混在一起的设计问题：学习率 cooldown 决定参数轨迹，最终模型选择器决定部署哪个点或哪个平均模型。传统做法直接返回最后一步参数，会偏好较保守的末端学习率；如果返回的是 checkpoint 平均，则更活跃的末端轨迹可能反而更好。","importance":"这是一个对预训练 recipe 有直接意义的工作。它把 cooldown schedule 和 output estimator 解耦，给出一个简单、低成本、可插入现有训练流程的末端估计器。贡献不在于提出复杂优化器，而在于改变了一个常见默认假设：末端学习率不应只按 raw final iterate 调参。对大模型训练中的 annealing、cooldown、checkpoint averaging、最终模型选择都有参考价值，尤其适合训练预算固定、不能大规模重训的场景。","technical_approach":"方法叫 Terminal Shrinkage Averaging，简称 TSA。设最后一步参数为 θ_T，最后 K 个 checkpoint 的均值为 \\bar{θ}_T，TSA 返回 (1-α)θ_T + α\\bar{θ}_T。α=0 是原始 final iterate，α=1 是 uniform LAWA；中间 α 允许在最近训练进展和末端随机波动之间做连续折中。因为 θ_T 也包含在平均中，最终 checkpoint 的权重是 1-α+α/K，其余 K-1 个 checkpoint 的权重是 α/K。理论分析使用末端短窗口的局部二次损失近似，用 curvature-weighted norm 表达风险，并把 checkpoint 分解为跨 run 的均值部分和随机残差。由此得到关于 α 的二次型 surrogate risk。这个式子清楚地区分两项：平均较旧 checkpoint 带来的均值位移，也就是 bias；以及平均多个 checkpoint 带来的 stochastic variation 降低，也就是 variance reduction。内点最优会在两者平衡时出现，因此 raw final iterate 和 LAWA 都不一定最优。进一步的推论是，使用平均估计器时，更高的 terminal learning-rate floor 可以保留末端优化进展，而平均操作负责抑制末端噪声；直接返回最后一步时，同样的高 floor 往往只表现为更大的终点方差。","experiments":"实验围绕两个变量做隔离：terminal learning-rate floor 和 output estimator。深度 12 的 NanoChat 实验先显示，最优 shrinkage coefficient 严格落在 α=0 和 α=1 之间，说明简单 raw final iterate 与完整 LAWA 都不是最佳端点。随后采用 paired factorial design，把末端学习率 floor 设为 peak LR 的 5%、10%、15%，并交叉比较 raw final iterate 与 TSA；每个配置做 5 次重复。结果方向很清楚：提高 floor 会让 raw final iterate 的 validation BPB 变差，但会让 TSA 的 validation BPB 变好，而且从 5% 到 10%、15% 的增幅越大，交互效应越明显。作者还用 pure AdamW 复现实验方向，说明现象不依赖 NanoChat 默认的 Muon+AdamW 组合。最后把更高 floor 与 TSA 应用到深度 22 NanoChat 的 time-to-GPT-2 recipe，在相同训练预算下优于对应公开 recipe，并得到一次符合 benchmark 规则的记录 run。给定材料没有提供具体 BPB 数值表，因此不宜补写精确提升幅度。","reading_guide":"建议精读。先看 Introduction 中对 schedule 与 estimator 的拆分，这是理解贡献的入口。Method 部分要重点看 TSA 定义、θ_T 在平均中的权重、局部二次近似、risk 关于 α 的二次形式，以及 bias-variance 分解。实验部分重点找三类图表：α sweep，5%/10%/15% floor 与 raw/TSA 的 factorial comparison，pure AdamW 复现实验。深度 22 结果用于判断能否迁移到更接近实际 recipe 的规模，但不应只看最终分数；更重要的是 schedule-estimator interaction 是否仍然存在。值得深挖的问题包括：K 和 checkpoint 间隔 s 如何选，TSA 与 EMA/SWA/LAWA 的差别，是否能在更长 cooldown、更大模型、不同数据顺序和不同 optimizer 下保持同样方向。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper separates two choices that are usually bundled together in LLM pretraining: the terminal learning-rate schedule that generates the late-stage parameter trajectory, and the estimator used to produce the deployed model from that trajectory. Returning the raw final iterate implicitly favors a quiet cooldown, while checkpoint averaging can make a more active terminal trajectory preferable.","importance":"This is directly relevant to pretraining practice. The main contribution is a clean recipe-level insight: cooldown tuning should be done jointly with the final-model estimator. A raw endpoint and an averaged endpoint can prefer different terminal learning-rate floors. The proposed estimator is simple enough to add to existing training runs, so the work is useful for annealing, cooldown design, checkpoint averaging, and final checkpoint selection under fixed training budgets.","technical_approach":"$b","experiments":"$c","reading_guide":"Read it closely if you work on pretraining schedules. Think of training as climbing toward the same capability target. The summit is the shared validation target, each route is a different batch size, and each marker is one optimizer update. After learning-rate retuning, B, 2B, and 4B make similar progress per response, while higher throughput lets the larger batches reach the summit sooner. At 16B, however, the additional sample cost outweighs the throughput gain, so the apparently larger step becomes a slower route. Recent systems achieve strong results with increasingly elaborate evolutionary search harnesses or by updating model parameters during test-time training. We introduce Hill Sampling, a simple procedure that repeatedly samples candidate program edits from a frozen LLM, retains the best program found so","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-22T00:08:12Z","category_id":"scaling","cover_url":"/covers/2609_25510.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作讨论的是一个很具体但很有代表性的问题：在“可验证程序发现”这类 test-time scaling 场景里，真的需要复杂的进化搜索、档案池、交叉变异、甚至 test-time training，才能把冻结 LLM 的额外推理算力转成更好的最优解吗？作者把目标函数说得很清楚：这里关心的不是平均样本质量，而是在固定预算内找到的最高 verified reward。对这类 heavy-tail 目标，很多会提升均值的方法，未必能提升最好一次命中的概率。论文的核心贡献，就是把问题还原到最小机制后发现：只保留“当前最优程序”这一份持久状态，并让后续采样都条件化在它上面，就已经能显著超过 repeated sampling、权重扰动搜索和大部分更复杂的搜索装置。","importance":"对基座研究的直接启发不在于它提出了新的预训练方法，而在于它给 test-time scaling 提供了一个很干净的对照结论：在可验证任务上，token-level sampling 本身就是很强的探索源；相比之下，直接在模型权重空间做随机扰动或 ES 学习，未必更适合追求 maximum return。这对“预训练模型内部是否天然密集分布着可用专家”“测试时是否该优先做参数适配还是输出空间搜索”这类问题都有实证价值。另一个重要点是，它把很多近来被默认需要的机制逐一拿掉后，仍能拿到很强结果，因此对后续工作有方法学上的约束作用：如果一个复杂系统声称有效，至少要先赢过这种极简 best-so-far conditioned sampling 基线。","technical_approach":"方法非常简单，但设计上抓住了目标函数。设初始程序为 x0，每一轮以当前 incumbent xt 为条件，从冻结 LLM 采样 N 个“程序编辑”，逐个执行 verifier 得到 reward；若本轮最好候选优于或等于历史最优，就把它设为新的 incumbent，并把该程序作为下一轮上下文。作者显式保留 incumbent 的历史最好 reward，而不是每轮重评估 incumbent，这样即便程序执行有随机性，也能保证 best-so-far 单调不降。接受 equal-reward 候选是为了保留一定结构多样性。整个算法没有 archive、没有 crossover、没有显式 diversity objective、没有参数更新，超参数基本只有 temperature、每轮样本数 N、轮数 M。\n\n论文的技术分析重点不只是提出 Hill Sampling，还系统比较了几类替代探索机制。第一类是 repeated sampling：每次都从同一个初始程序独立采样，不把已发现的好程序反馈给后续采样。Hill Sampling 相比它多出的唯一机制，就是“把最好程序写回上下文”。第二类是直接对 LLM 权重做 evolution strategies。作者实现了大规模 ES 管线，并区分了正常 ES 学习与 learning-rate=0 的纯噪声扰动版本。这个对照很关键，因为它把“权重扰动带来的探索”与“权重更新带来的学习”拆开了。结果显示，ES 学习常常提高 mean return，却压低 max return；而 lr=0 的模型噪声有时能帮助探索，但仍弱于原始模型的 token sampling 随机性。第三类是各种常见增强件，如鼓励多样性、组合多个解、加入执行反馈等，作者都做了实现并报告这些机制在该设定下不是必要条件。\n\n从机制上看，Hill Sampling 有点像把 LLM 当作条件编辑器，而不是一次性生成器。incumbent 提供了一个不断上升的局部支架，模型后续采样不再从问题描述重新开始，而是在“当前最好程序附近”做局部搜索。它不像传统进化算法那样维护种群，也不像 test-time training 那样改参数，而是把搜索压力放在上下文条件化上。这也是它简单但有效的原因。","experiments":"实验覆盖三个可验证数学/算法发现任务和三个开源模型：circle packing 使用 gpt-oss-20b；sums/differences of finite sets 使用 OLMo-3.1-32B-Instruct；Erdős minimum-overlap problem 使用 Mistral-Small-3.1-24B-Instruct。作者强调的是 practical wall-clock budget，而不是只比 sample efficiency。\n\n最强结果出现在 circle packing：Hill Sampling 在 8 张 H100 上不到 5 小时拿到已发表方法中的最好结果。对 Erdős minimum-overlap，它在 12 小时内超过 AlphaEvolve 的 algorithmic-discovery reference。摘要截断了部分数字，但正文给出的结论很明确：Hill Sampling 在至少一个任务上刷新公开结果，在另外两个任务上也达到强竞争水平。\n\n消融的价值很高。首先，Hill Sampling 明显优于 repeated sampling，说明“best-so-far conditioning”本身就是主要增益来源。其次，ES learning 往往提升平均回报却降低最大回报，这和作者前面强调的目标函数差异一致：如果任务看重 rare high-reward discovery，优化均值可能反而伤害尾部。再次，把 ES 学习率设为 0 后，纯权重噪声有时比带学习的 ES 更能保住最大值，但仍不如不扰动权重、直接靠 token 采样探索。这个结果很值得注意，因为它等于在一个可精确验证的环境里，给“权重空间附近有很多可用专家”这个命题加了边界条件：即便存在，找到它们也未必比输出空间搜索更划算。\n\n作者还比较了 N=64 与 N=512 两种 Hill Sampling 配置，在相同总采样预算下改变每轮并行度与轮数的分配。这个设计是在问：是更频繁地更新 incumbent 更重要，还是单轮看更多候选更重要。虽然截取内容里没有完整表格数字，但从方法描述和结论看，作者认为条件化链式改进是关键，因此轮次结构本身是值得关注的实验维度。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先抓三处。第一看 Introduction 前半段，作者把目标从“提升平均质量”切到“提升 fixed-budget 下的最大 verified reward”，这是理解全文的钥匙。第二看 Methods 里 Hill Sampling 的正式定义，尤其是 incumbent reward 的保存方式、equal-reward acceptance，以及 N=64/N=512 的预算控制。第三看对 ES 与 model-noise 的实验部分，这部分不是陪衬，而是在回答一个更一般的问题：测试时探索到底该发生在 token 空间还是权重空间。\n\n如果只花 20 到 30 mean return 的张力讲得很清楚，这对很多 RL、best-of-N、verifier-guided decoding 工作都有直接启发。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a narrow but important question in test-time scaling for verifiable discovery: how much machinery is actually needed to turn extra inference compute into better best-found solutions? Recent systems rely on evolutionary search stacks, archives, crossover, or even test-time training. This work reframes the objective carefully: the target is not average sample quality, but the maximum verified reward found under a fixed budget. Under that objective, methods that improve the mean can still hurt the chance of hitting a rare high-reward solution. The central finding is that a minimal procedure—keep only the best verified program so far and condition future edits on it—is already enough to outperform repeated sampling, weight-space exploration, and several more elaborate alternatives.","importance":"Its value for foundation-model research is methodological rather than pretraining-specific. It provides a clean empirical result about where useful exploration comes from at test time. In these verifiable program-discovery settings, token-level sampling from a frozen model is a stronger source of diversity than random perturbations in weight space, and often better aligned with the max-return objective than ES-style learning. That matters for ongoing discussions around dense local experts in pretrained weight space, parameter adaptation at inference time, and whether output-space search should be the default baseline before adding model updates. Just as important, the paper establishes a strong minimal baseline. A standard acceleration strategy is block-wise decoding, where each forward pass predicts a block of length B and commits high-confidence tokens. However, B couples two distinct decisions: the look-ahead horizon and the number of tokens to commit. Existing accelerators address this limitation through indirect heuristics","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-13T05:12:09Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2609_26249.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"解决 diffusion language model 在块式并行解码中，前瞻长度与提交 token 数被同一个块大小 B 绑死，导致加速与质量难以同时兼顾的问题。","importance":"这属于文本生成范式层面的推理机制改进。价值不在于再做一个启发式加速器，而是在 dLLM 的 block decoding 里把“看多远”和“交多少”两个决策拆开，并声称可直接从模型自身置信度曲线中读出合适的前瞻范围。","technical_approach":"提出 PACE-dLLM。核心观察是：窗口内逐步置信度通常呈现上下文相关的 cliff 形状，达到饱和的位置可作为下一步 look-ahead horizon。方法在每一步对该置信度 cliff 做闭式参数拟合，用饱和点决定前瞻长度，再用独立的置信度阈值决定实际提交多少 token，从而解耦 horizon 与 commit。相比 volatility tracking、delimiter detection、learned scoring 等间接启发式，它直接利用模型内生的 per-step confidence 信号。","experiments":"摘要未给出完整实验表，但定位很明确：在 LLaDA、Dream 这类 dLLM 上评估生成质量与解码效率，比较对象应包括固定块长和现有自适应 block-wise decoding 启发式。需要重点看是否在相同质量下显著减少 decoding steps，以及在不同上下文类型下 cliff 拟合是否稳定；若只在少数 prompt 模式有效，结论会偏弱。","authors_note":"作者来自香港科技大学、西北工业大学、香港理工大学。不是头部实验室，但题目切中 dLLM 推理机制这个当前仍在快速摸索的空白带。"},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a core limitation of block-wise decoding for diffusion language models: the block size B entangles two different decisions, how far to look ahead and how many tokens to commit.","importance":"This is relevant at the modeling-paradigm level rather than as a generic inference trick. The main value is the claim that dLLMs already expose a usable confidence structure for adaptive decoding, instead of relying on external heuristics.","technical_approach":"PACE-dLLM is built on the observation that in-window confidence often follows a context-dependent cliff. The method fits this cliff in closed form at each decoding step, uses the saturation point to set the next look-ahead horizon, and applies an independent confidence threshold to decide token commitment. This explicitly decouples horizon selection from commit length.","experiments":"The abstract does not provide full numbers, but the intended setup is clear: evaluate on dLLMs such as LLaDA and Dream, compare against fixed-block decoding and prior adaptive heuristics, and measure both generation quality and decoding efficiency. Unlike open-ended text generation, OCR outputs are strongly grounded in the input image, making diffusion-based parallel generation promising. However, when several tokens are predicted in one diffusion step, each is predicted before the others are known. Committing them directly can therefore introduce ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-22T16:11:47Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2609_26638.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是一个很具体但有代表性的问题：文档 OCR 的生成式模型大多仍是严格自回归解码，输出一长段文本、HTML 表格或 LaTeX 时，延迟几乎线性受 token 数限制。OCR 与开放式文本生成不同，很多位置其实被图像强约束，适合并行生成；但如果直接用 block diffusion 一次提交多个 token，又会因为块内 token 彼此不可见，破坏阅读顺序、标签闭合和结构一致性。论文要解决的不是“能不能用 diffusion 做 OCR”，而是“怎样在不引入独立 drafter、尽量保留原 AR 模型精度的前提下，把并行 drafting 和可靠 commitment 结合起来”。","importance":"对基座预训练研究的意义主要有三层。第一，它给出了一种参数共享的 AR+diffusion 混合建模方案，说明同一套权重可以同时承担 block-level parallel drafting 和 token-level causal verification，这对统一建模范式很有参考价值，不只限于 OCR。第二，它把 self-speculative decoding 从常见的“小模型起草、大模型验证”改成“同模型双路径”，避免了额外 drafter 的训练和部署复杂度，这对 serving 与训练一体化设计有启发。第三，它展示了一个很实际的 pretrain↔posttrain 接口：最终输出由 AR 路径决定，因此可以只沿 AR 路径做 GRPO，用序列级和结构级奖励优化共享参数，而不必给 diffusion 轨迹显式建 likelihood。量级上，这不是通用语言建模范式的根本替代，但在多模态生成、结构化输出和 grounded generation 场景里，属于值得认真看的工程型方法论文。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 GravityOCR，一个参数共享的 AR–block-diffusion 模型。它从已有的 GLM-OCR 出发，把同一个 decoder 置于两种注意力模式下工作。第一条路径是 block diffusion drafter：把输出序列按长度不超过 B 的连续块分块，块间保持从左到右的因果依赖，块内使用双向注意力；训练时对当前块随机 mask 若干位置，学习在给定已完成前缀和部分可见块内容的条件下恢复被 mask token。推理时，当前块先全 mask，再通过若干 denoising step 并行恢复多个位置，得到一个 draft block。第二条路径是 causal AR verifier：对同一前缀下的 draft 逐 token 验证，只接受与 AR greedy 预测一致的最长前缀；一旦出现分歧，就在该位置停止提交，回到下一轮 drafting。这样做的关键点在于，最终 committed 序列由 AR 路径裁决，因此在精确算术下、采用 top-1 verification 时，输出可与纯 AR greedy 保持一致；diffusion 只负责争取一次多走几步，而不直接决定最终 token。\n\n论文强调它不是传统 speculative decoding。传统做法通常需要一个单独的小 drafter，或者额外的 multi-token head；这里 drafting 和 verification 共用完整 backbone，没有辅助头，也没有第二个模型。训练上，作者先保留 AR NLL，再加入 block diffusion denoising 目标，使模型学会在两种 attention mask 下工作。后续再通过 GRPO 只沿 causal AR path 注入 OCR reward，包括序列级与结构级信号。这样做的原因很明确：最终输出质量由 verifier 决定，reward 也应作用在真正决定输出的路径上；同时避免为 diffusion 轨迹设计复杂的 credit assignment。文中还比较了直接 block-diffusion 提交与 self-speculative verification 的差异：前者并行度更高，但容易在表格标签、公式边界、跨 token 依赖处出错；后者牺牲一部分并行度，换来接近 AR 的稳定性。","experiments":"实验基于 GLM-OCR 改造，并在 OmniDocBench v1.6 上评估。最关键的结果有四个。第一，最终模型在 Overall 分数上基本保留原始 AR 模型能力：95.16 对 95.48，保留约 99.7% 的性能；这说明共享 diffusion drafter 并没有明显伤害主路径质量。第二，只沿 AR 路径做 GRPO 能把分数从 94.92 提升到 95.16，说明 reward 优化确实能改善最终 verifier 决定的输出，同时不会破坏 drafter 的可用性。第三，在 SGLang 中实现完整 self-speculative decoding 后，达到 9.7 tokens per forward，且这里把 diffusion drafting forward 和 AR verification forward 都计入了；相对纯 causal AR，token generation speedup 为 3.94×，端到端 page processing speedup 为 1.32×。这两个数字的差距也很重要：说明系统瓶颈并不只在 decoder token loop，视觉编码、I/O、后处理等仍占了不小比例。第四，论文系统分析了 accuracy–parallelism trade-off：直接 block diffusion 在并行提交上更激进，但精度下降更明显；加入 verifier 后，accepted draft length 受 draft–verifier 一致率控制，结构化 OCR 任务里这个一致率决定了真实加速上限。\n\n从消融角度看，值得注意的点包括：块大小 B、denoising 步数、验证策略、以及不同文档子任务上的收益差异。结构更强、局部可由图像直接约束的 plain text OCR 更容易接受长 draft；而 HTML 表格和 LaTeX 公式因为存在更强的 token 间依赖，draft prefix 更容易被 verifier 截断。文中还提到在 bf16 serving kernel 下测量与 AR greedy 的一致性，这一点很务实：理论上的 exact arithmetic 等价，在实际部署里会受到数值误差和 kernel 实现影响，作者至少意识到了这一层。","reading_guide":"泛读时建议先抓三处。第一看引言和 Figure 2，理解为什么“图像强约束”并不等于“可以直接并行提交”，块内 token 互相不可见会怎样破坏结构一致性。第二看方法部分里 block-causal attention 与 longest-consistent-prefix verification 的定义，这是整篇工作的真正创新点。第三看实验里关于 TPF、3.94× token speedup、1.32× page speedup 以及 95.16 vs 95.48 的表格和图，判断它到底是算法收益还是系统收益。\n\n如果要多花一点时间，建议重点看附录里关于 top-1 verification 与 AR greedy 一致性的论证，以及 bf16 下实际一致率的测量方式。另一个值得深挖的点是 GRPO 只走 AR path 的设计：这相当于把 post-training 信号通过共享参数反哺 diffusion drafter，但没有直接优化 drafting objective。对做统一生成模型的人，这里有一个开放问题：如果 verifier 长期主导训练，drafter 会不会逐渐退化成“只会给 verifier 提供容易接受的保守草稿”，从而限制并行潜力。预计 20–30 分钟可以完成有效泛读。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific but important bottleneck in generative document OCR: most strong OCR VLMs still decode strictly autoregressively, so latency scales almost linearly with output length. OCR is more grounded than open-ended text generation, which makes parallel generation attractive. But direct block-diffusion commitment is unreliable because tokens predicted within the same block cannot condition on one another, leading to ordering errors, malformed tags, and structural inconsistencies. The real problem here is not whether diffusion can be used for OCR, but how to combine parallel drafting with reliable commitment while preserving the accuracy of an existing AR model and avoiding a separate drafter.","importance":"Its value for pretraining-oriented readers is threefold. First, it presents a parameter-shared AR+diffusion hybrid where one backbone supports both block-level parallel drafting and token-level causal verification. That is relevant beyond OCR as a concrete recipe for unified generative modeling. Second, it turns speculative decoding into a self-speculative setup: no small external drafter, no auxiliary prediction head, and no second model to train or serve. Third, it shows a practical pretrain-to-posttrain interface: because final outputs are decided by the AR path, GRPO can be applied only through that path using sequence- and structure-level OCR rewards, without defining likelihoods over diffusion trajectories. This is not a general replacement for AR language modeling, but it is a serious systems-and-modeling paper for grounded generation and structured outputs.","technical_approach":"$10","experiments":"$11","reading_guide":"$12","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"将 Diffusion drafting 和 AR verification 融合在同一个参数共享模型中，并巧妙利用 AR 路径做 GRPO 绕开 diffusion RL 的痛点，这种 AR+Diffusion 联合建模的思路对探索 next-token 之外的生成范式很有启发。","en":"Fuses Diffusion drafting and AR verification within a single parameter-shared model, cleverly using the AR path for GRPO to bypass diffusion RL pain points. We prove that LEMA transformers with chain of thought can simulate word-RAMs, as was recently shown for the less restrictive rightmost hard attention. In contrast to prior hard attention variants, the restriction to exact matches enables an efficient converse direction: word-RAMs can simulate LEMA transformers","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-22T07:36:00Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2609_25802.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CC","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文提出 Latest Exact Match Attention，也就是把 query/key 二值化后，只检索最近一个完全匹配 key 的 value，目标是在保留可增长记忆的同时，让自回归生成的每 token 查找成本不随上下文长度增长。","importance":"这篇更偏架构与理论，但和长上下文预训练、KV-cache 设计、非 softmax attention 的可训练性直接相关。它给出一个很干净的计算模型：softmax attention 是连续相似度检索，LEMA 则是离散字典检索。好处是推理时每个 head 的 cache 可以实现为 dictionary，每步只做 lookup 和 insert；代价是训练困难，因为二值化和“最近精确匹配”不可微。论文的价值不在于当前语言建模结果已经成熟，而在于把可增长外部状态、常数时间检索、Transformer 表达能力和 word-RAM 模型放到同一个框架里，提供了一个可实验的注意力替代方向。","technical_approach":"LEMA head 先用线性层生成 query 和 key，再通过 sign 函数二值化到 {-1, 1}，value 仍是连续向量。对当前位置的 query，它只返回历史中最近的、key 完全相同的位置的 value；如果没有匹配，则返回零向量。推理时，每个 attention head 的 KV cache 可以看作从二进制 key 到最近 value 的字典，插入新 key 时覆盖旧值，因此每 token 的核心查找成本与上下文长度无关。理论部分证明了两个方向：带 chain-of-thought 的 LEMA Transformer 可以模拟 word-RAM；反过来，word-RAM 也可以用每 token O(N) 步模拟一个 N 参数 LEMA Transformer，空间随 distinct keys 数量增长。训练部分使用 straight-through estimator 处理 sign 二值化，用 stick-breaking attention 的 surrogate 近似 latest-match 操作，并逐渐退火到 LEMA。注意训练时仍是序列长度二次复杂度，效率优势主要在推理路径。","experiments":"实验分两类。第一类是 synthetic associative recall，模型需要记住越来越多 token 关联并回答查询。小型 LEMA Transformer 能像 softmax Transformer 一样存储大量关联，而 gated DeltaNet 在关联数量增大后失败。第二类是在 FineWeb-Edu 上训练最大 834M 参数的 LEMA 语言模型。较大规模下，LEMA 的语言建模 loss 大致能匹配参数量略多于其一半的 softmax Transformer，但仍落后于同等规模 softmax。长程召回代理任务上，包括 repeated rare phrases loss 和 RULER single-needle with repetitive filler，LEMA 仍明显弱于 softmax，但在长距离上优于 GDN。作者也指出训练方法还有缺陷，当前结果更像是验证可训练性和推理结构，而不是给出可直接替换 softmax attention 的 recipe。","reading_guide":"泛读建议 35–45 分钟。先看 Definition 1 和 Figure 1，理解 LEMA 如何把 KV-cache 变成字典。再看 Theorem 1 和 Theorem 2 的陈述，不必深追全部证明，但要理解为什么 exact match 使得 converse simulation 比 rightmost hard attention 更自然。方法部分重点看 straight-through estimator、stick-breaking surrogate 和 annealing，因为这是从理论 attention 走向可训练模型的关键。实验部分优先看 associative recall、FineWeb-Edu scaling 曲线、repeated rare phrases、RULER single-needle。值得深挖的问题是：二值 key 的碰撞、训练 surrogate 与推理规则的 mismatch、以及 exact-match 检索是否能在真实预训练中学到稳定可泛化的地址空间。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper introduces Latest Exact Match Attention, where binary queries and keys retrieve only the most recent value with an exactly matching key. The goal is to keep a growing memory while making autoregressive lookup independent of context length.","importance":"This is mainly an architecture and theory paper, but it is relevant to long-context pretraining and KV-cache design. LEMA turns attention into a discrete dictionary lookup rather than a soft similarity scan. During inference, each head can store a map from binary keys to the latest value, so each token needs one lookup and one insert rather than a scan over all past tokens. The current language modeling results do not make it a drop-in replacement for softmax attention. The contribution is a sharper framework for thinking about expandable memory, constant-time retrieval, Transformer expressivity, and trainable non-softmax attention.","technical_approach":"A LEMA head computes linear queries and keys, binarizes them with a sign function into {-1, 1}, and keeps continuous values. For a given query, the head returns the value at the latest previous position whose binary key exactly matches the query; if no such key exists, it returns zeros. The KV cache can be implemented as a dictionary, where inserting an already-seen key overwrites the old value. The theory gives two simulations. First, LEMA Transformers with chain-of-thought can simulate word-RAM computations. Second, a word-RAM can simulate autoregressive generation of an N-parameter LEMA Transformer in O(N) steps per token, with memory tied to the number of distinct keys rather than the context length. For training, the authors use a straight-through estimator for query/key binarization and a stick-breaking attention surrogate for the non-differentiable latest-match operation, annealing the surrogate toward LEMA. Training remains quadratic in sequence length; the efficiency argument applies to inference.","experiments":"The experiments start with synthetic associative recall, where the model must store many token associations and answer later queries. Small LEMA Transformers can retain many associations, similar to softmax Transformers, while gated DeltaNet fails as the number of associations grows. The authors then train LEMA language models up to 834M parameters on FineWeb-Edu. At larger scales, LEMA reaches a language modeling loss comparable to softmax Transformers with slightly more than half as many parameters, but it still trails same-size softmax models. On long-range recall proxies, including repeated rare phrase loss and RULER single-needle with repetitive filler, LEMA remains clearly behind softmax attention, though it performs better than GDN at long distances. The results show feasibility, but also expose a training gap between the surrogate objective and the hard inference rule.","reading_guide":"A 35–45 minute skim is appropriate. Start with Definition 1 and Figure 1 to understand how the KV cache becomes a dictionary. Then read the statements of Theorem 1 and Theorem 2; the key point is that exact-match lookup enables an efficient converse simulation that is harder to obtain for broader hard-attention rules. In the training section, focus on the straight-through estimator, the stick-breaking surrogate, and the annealing schedule. In the experiments, inspect associative recall, the FineWeb-Edu scaling results, repeated rare phrases, and RULER single-needle. The main open questions are binary-key collisions, mismatch between the training surrogate and inference rule, and whether exact-match addressing can learn robust retrieval structures in large-scale pretraining.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"只看理论主结论和训练 surrogate；大规模 LM 证据还没有，但 exact-match attention 的计算对应关系值得记住。","en":"Read the main theory and the training surrogate; large-scale LM evidence is absent, but the exact-match attention correspondence is worth retaining."},"metadata":{"institutions":["University of Tübingen","Affiliation:Department of Mathematics, University of Tübingen"],"authors":["Moritz Brösamle"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["attention","hard-attention","theory","associative-recall"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25802","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.25802","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.25655","title":"From Experts to Sub-experts: Fine-grained Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning for MoE LLMs","abstract":"As large language models (LLMs) scale rapidly, dense full-parameter adaptation becomes increasingly expensive, motivating sparse and modular architectures such as Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models. This shift raises a key question for parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT): at what granularity should parameters be selected and updated? Existing PEFT methods such as LoRA operate on predefined weight matrices, while expert-level sparse tuning methods update entire selected experts. That flexibility helps correct earlier mistakes, but it also creates a failure mode where tokens that were already correct get overwritten later. The authors turn this into a measurable phenomenon: in a Sudoku diagnostic, 9.4% of cells are correct at some intermediate step but wrong in the final output. The core problem is therefore how to introduce a sensible generation order into uniform discrete flow, without retraining the model, so that reliable predictions can be fixed early and protected from late-stage corruption.","importance":"The direct impact on pretraining is more indirect than a new training objective, but the paper is quite relevant for non-autoregressive language modeling and discrete diffusion/flow paradigms. 观察翻转步骤的误差形态，layer grafting 测试不同层替换对翻转的影响，KV-cache grafting 排除缓存轨迹差异的部分解释，logit-lens traceback 追踪中间表示到输出 logit 的变化。关键点是：transformer body 的 hidden-state error 会累积，但这种累积本身不能区分会翻转和不会翻转的步骤；真正决定翻转的是 lm_head locality。第二步利用 token-level sparsity：虽然每一步看起来处理全序列，但真正影响当前预测分布的 token 只是一部分，因此 cache 复用和验证应优先服务这些高影响 token，而不是均匀处理所有缓存状态。第三步是 KV-cache 驱动的 parallel draft-and-verify：不引入额外 drafter，也不训练辅助模型，而是让 dLLM 自己根据缓存上下文提出多个候选 token，再用更可靠的置信度进行并行验证和提交。它和 AR speculative decoding 的区别在于，AR 的 cache 是前缀单调增长，dLLM 的 cache 来自反复重访和局部更新，关键难点是如何避免全序列 cache 反复搬运。","experiments":"所给内容主要呈现了动机实验和系统观察。作者报告了三类现象：第一，启用 KV cache 后，冗余读写可能成为主要运行时瓶颈，说明 FLOPs 下降不一定转化为 wall-clock 加速；第二，dLLM 解码中存在明显 token 稀疏性，只有一小部分已解码或部分解码 token 对当前预测有显著贡献；第三，候选 token 的平均置信度越高，每个去噪步骤能被正确接受的 token 数越多。论文据此声称 Flash-dLLM 能在保持生成质量的同时减少去噪步数，并提升速度和显存效率。当前提供的全文片段没有包含完整 benchmark 表、具体模型规模、序列长度、吞吐提升或显存下降数字，精读时需要核对实验章节中的端到端 latency、per-step kernel time、cache traffic 统计和质量保持指标。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 20 到 30 分钟。重点看 Introduction 中对 dLLM KV cache 和 memory I/O 的瓶颈拆解，尤其是 Fig. 1 中关于读写冗余、token 影响稀疏性和置信度动态的证据。方法部分应重点看 fused KV-cache kernel 如何定义缓存读写边界，以及 parallel draft-and-verify 如何决定候选 token、验证 token 和提交 token。实验部分需要核对三个问题：加速来自减少去噪步数还是减少单步 I/O；不同长度、batch size、denoising steps 下收益是否稳定；质量指标是否覆盖长文本一致性，而不只是短文本困惑度或匹配率。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a concrete deployment bottleneck in diffusion LLMs. Although dLLMs can in principle decode non-autoregressively, their iterative denoising process repeatedly revisits the full sequence. KV caching is therefore less straightforward than in autoregressive decoding: cache reads, writes, and partial updates can dominate runtime once cache reuse and parallel verification are used together.","importance":"The useful point for pretraining researchers is that dLLM viability depends on more than the denoising objective. The inference behavior is tightly coupled with the generation schedule, token update sparsity, and confidence calibration. A diffusion LM that looks attractive at the modeling level may still be hard to serve if its denoising trajectory forces heavy cache traffic. The contribution is mainly an inference and decoding framework, not a new pretraining recipe, but it gives concrete signals for designing cache-aware diffusion language models.","technical_approach":"Flash-dLLM is a training-free acceleration framework. It first introduces an IO-aware fused KV-cache mechanism to reduce redundant GPU memory movement and improve cache locality during iterative denoising. It then exploits token-level sparsity: even though a denoising step processes the whole sequence, only a subset of cached tokens meaningfully changes the current prediction distribution. Finally, it adds a KV-cache-based parallel draft-and-verify procedure in which the dLLM acts as both drafter and verifier, without an auxiliary draft model or extra training. This differs from standard autoregressive KV caching because AR caches grow monotonically with the prefix, while dLLM caches are repeatedly revisited and partially updated.","experiments":"The provided content mainly reports motivating measurements rather than full benchmark tables. The authors identify three empirical patterns: redundant KV-cache reads and writes can dominate runtime; only a small subset of decoded tokens strongly affects the current denoising step; and higher average confidence among candidate tokens leads to more correct token acceptances per step. The paper claims that the proposed method improves speed and memory efficiency while preserving generation quality. The available text does not include full numbers for model size, sequence length, throughput, latency, memory reduction, or quality metrics, so those need to be checked in the experimental section.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20 to 30 minutes. Focus on the bottleneck analysis in the introduction, especially the figures on cache I/O, token sparsity, and confidence dynamics. In the method section, check how the fused KV-cache kernel defines read/write/update boundaries, and how the draft-and-verify procedure selects, verifies, and commits tokens. In the experiments, the key questions are whether the speedup comes from fewer denoising steps or cheaper steps, whether it holds across context lengths and batch sizes, and whether quality is measured beyond short-form generation.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"关注 Diffusion LM 落地效率的必读工作，将 KV Cache 和并行解码引入 dLLM 并解决了 I/O 瓶颈，证明了非自回归模型在推理速度上确实有潜力打败 AR。","en":"A must-read for those tracking the deployment efficiency of Diffusion LMs. By introducing KV caching and parallel decoding to dLLMs and solving the I/O bottleneck, it proves that NAR models have the potential to beat AR in inference speed."},"metadata":{"institutions":["MBZUAI","Affiliation:VILA Lab, MBZUAI","Affiliation:Abu Dhabi"],"authors":["Quan Nguyen-Tri","Mukul Ranjan","Zhiqiang Shen"],"notable_authors":["Zhiqiang Shen"],"tags":["Diffusion LLM","Inference Acceleration","KV Cache","Parallel Decoding"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26796","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.26796","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.26425","title":"QuantWM: Temporally Consistent 2-Bit KV Cache Quantization for World Models and Video Generation","abstract":"KV cache memory has become a major deployment bottleneck for video generation and world models, which motivates low-bit quantization study for efficiency. to","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-22T13:50:54Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2609_26425.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是视频生成和 world model 推理阶段的一个被低估的问题：2-bit KV cache 量化在常规视频 benchmark 上看起来几乎无损，但实际生成视频会出现明显的时间闪烁、模糊和伪影。作者进一步指出，问题主要不在 Value，而在 Key。更反直觉的是，Key 的重建误差明明比 Value 更小，却造成更大的输出退化。论文要解决的核心，就是如何在极低比特 KV 压缩下保住注意力选择行为，尤其是跨时间的 token 选择稳定性。","importance":"这项工作不属于传统文本 LLM 预训练，但对基座研究仍有启发，原因在于它把 KV cache 压缩问题从“张量误差最小化”重新表述成“注意力行为保持”。这个视角很重要。无论是长上下文语言模型、视频生成还是 world model，缓存压缩真正伤害的往往不是数值本身，而是 QK^T 排序被扰动后，模型开始看错历史位置。对于做统一多模态 LM、长序列推理、推理系统优化的人，这篇文章提供了一个更接近机制层的分析框架：Key 比 Value 更敏感，不是因为量化误差大，而是因为它直接决定检索路径。","technical_approach":"方法是一个 training-free 的 2-bit KV cache 量化框架 QuantWM，核心目标不是单纯降低 Key/Value 重建误差，而是显式保护 attention logits 和时间一致性。它建立在 QVG 的 centroid-residual 表示之上：每个 Key 先分配到一个 BF16 centroid，再把 residual 做低比特量化。作者认为 QVG 的问题在于 centroid 选择主要由 K-means 决定，优化的是几何距离，而不是量化后对 QK^T 的影响。\n\n第一部分是 QSAC，quantization-sensitivity-aware clustering。它重新设计 centroid 选择标准，不再只看 Key 在特征空间里的聚类误差，而是联合考虑 Query 敏感性和 residual 动态范围，目标是让 INT2 residual 量化后对 attention logits 的扰动更小。直观上，某些 Key 方向虽然欧氏误差不大，但如果正好落在 Query 高敏感方向上，就会显著改变 token 排序；QSAC 就是在聚类阶段提前规避这类“对注意力很危险”的残差分布。\n\n第二部分是 PSAC，principal-subspace attention compensation。即便做了更好的聚类，Key 量化误差仍会沿着某些 Query 主方向放大到 logits 里。PSAC 的做法是在推理时提取主导 Query 子空间，用低秩投影去表示并补偿 Key 误差在这些敏感方向上的分量。可以把它理解为：不是试图完整恢复 Key，而是只修正那些最会改变注意力分配的误差分量。这和常见的“提高量化精度”路线不同，它承认 2-bit 下不可能全面保真，于是把有限预算用在最影响检索行为的子空间上。\n\n整套方法的逻辑很顺：先在量化前通过 QSAC 让 Key 更适合被 2-bit 表示，再在量化后通过 PSAC 对最关键的 attention 偏移做补偿。作者强调这是 training-free，因此部署门槛低，适合直接替换现有视频生成和 world model 的 KV 压缩模块。","experiments":"实验覆盖多类代表性模型，包括 LingBot-World-v2、Matrix-Game-2、LongCat-Video、HY-World 1.5 和 Causal-Forcing，说明作者希望证明方法不是绑定某一架构。论文给出的总体结论是：在保持 VBench 等常规指标竞争力的同时，QuantWM 明显缓解了 2-bit 量化带来的 temporal flickering、模糊和伪影，并实现最高 6.20× 的 KV cache 内存压缩，推理额外开销有限。\n\n更关键的是分析实验。作者先把 Key-only 和 Value-only 量化拆开，发现主要退化来自 Key；再比较重建误差，发现 Key 的数值误差反而更小。这组结果支撑了全文最重要的判断：不能再用“谁量化误差大谁更危险”的直觉看 KV cache，而要看它对 attention logits 和 token ranking 的影响。虽然给出的正文片段里没有完整表格数字，但从方法叙述可以看出，QSAC 和 PSAC 都是围绕这个诊断展开的，且目标不是单纯提升 benchmark 分数，而是修复 benchmark 不敏感但肉眼明显的时间一致性问题。","reading_guide":"泛读建议先看 Introduction，尤其是作者如何论证“benchmark 几乎不掉分，但视频明显变差”这一矛盾。然后直接看 Key vs Value 的拆分分析，这是整篇文章最值得记住的观察。方法部分重点看 QSAC 和 PSAC 各自解决哪一层问题：前者在量化表示阶段减少对 QK^T 的潜在伤害，后者在推理阶段补偿已经发生的敏感方向误差。实验部分优先看可视化对比、Key/Value 消融和压缩率-质量权衡。预计 20 分钟左右可以抓住主线；如果你做长上下文 LLM 或多模态缓存压缩，值得多花时间想一件事：文本模型里的 KV 量化是否也存在“指标稳定但检索路径漂移”的同类问题。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies an underappreciated failure mode of 2-bit KV-cache quantization in video generation and world models. Existing methods can look nearly lossless on standard benchmarks such as VBench, yet the generated videos still show obvious temporal flickering, blur, and artifacts. The deeper finding is that the main problem comes from quantizing Keys rather than Values. Even more surprisingly, Keys can have smaller reconstruction error than Values while causing much larger output degradation. The core problem, then, is how to preserve attention selection behavior under extreme KV compression, especially temporal token-selection stability.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper in the narrow text-LLM sense, but it is still relevant to foundation-model research because it reframes KV compression from tensor reconstruction to attention-behavior preservation. That shift matters. In long-context language models, video generators, and world models alike, the real damage from cache compression often comes not from raw numerical error, but from perturbing QK^T enough to change which past tokens are retrieved. For anyone working on unified multimodal models, long-sequence inference, or systems-aware modeling, the paper offers a mechanism-level lesson: Keys are more sensitive not because they are harder to quantize numerically, but because they define the retrieval path.","technical_approach":"$1b","experiments":"$1c","reading_guide":"For a quick read, start with the introduction and focus on the mismatch between benchmark stability and visible quality degradation. It partitions policies into vision encoder, multimodal projector, language backbone, and action head, with finer named layer groups and projection-level interventions. The main formats include W3, W4, W8, W4A4, W4A8, and W8A8, with additional W2 and activation-bit studies. The RTN experiments quantize and dequantize weights in place and quantize linear inputs through hooks, while computation remains floating point. This isolates accuracy effects from packed-weight memory savings or integer-kernel speedups. A notable result is a non-monotonic recovery: for π0.5 under uncalibrated W4A4 RTN, expanding an action-head subset from 126 to 167 layers raises success from 7.0% to 70.5%, and paired fixed-observation replay shows matching numerical recovery. Sparse attention is widely used to accelerate LLM inference by computing exact attention over a selected subset of tokens. To recover the contribution of tokens excluded from exact attention, recent methods apply coarse-grained compensation to the omitted attention tail. However, existing methods typically select tokens based on attention mass and only then co","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-22T12:11:19Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2609_26300.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"在长上下文推理中，现有的稀疏注意力机制通常先根据注意力权重选择KV块，然后再对未选中的块进行补偿。这种解耦设计忽略了补偿误差：被选中的块不一定是补偿后误差最大的块，导致近似精度受损。","importance":"提出了首个“补偿感知”（compensation-aware）的KV选择框架。通过将选择和补偿机制耦合，优化了长上下文推理中稀疏注意力的精度，为缓解KV Cache内存带宽瓶颈提供了一种更有效的免训练方案。","technical_approach":"提出CompKV，将全块注意力和基于摘要的近似注意力形式化为分布，其KL散度可简化为对数配分间隙。分析表明，未选中块产生的残差由其注意力权重（attention mass）和块内Logit方差（within-block logit variation）共同决定。CompKV为每个KV块维护紧凑的均值和分组方差统计量，在查询时估算补偿后的残差，并据此对块进行排序选择。此外，还开发了异步CPU卸载实现，以重叠计算和数据传输。","experiments":"在RULER和LongBench-Pro基准测试中，使用Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct、Qwen3-8B和Qwen3-32B模型，CompKV在所有评估的稀疏方法中取得了最高的平均准确率。在NVIDIA H100上的效率测试中（32K-128K上下文，512-2048 Token预算），CompKV实现了最低的平均解码步延迟，相比全注意力机制最高加速6.85倍。","reading_guide":"建议重点阅读“补偿感知选择公式”（Compensation-Aware Selection Formulation）部分，理解注意力权重和块内方差如何共同影响补偿残差。图1直观展示了CompKV与现有方法的区别。关注实验部分中CompKV在不同上下文长度和Token预算下的精度-效率权衡。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"In long-context inference, existing sparse attention mechanisms typically select KV blocks based on attention mass first, and then compensate for the unselected blocks. This decoupled design ignores the compensation error: the selected blocks are not necessarily those that would leave the largest error if compensated, leading to compromised approximation accuracy.","importance":"Introduces the first 'compensation-aware' KV selection framework. This matters for preserving post-trained reasoning ability under aggressive compression, especially for edge or bandwidth-constrained serving.","technical_approach":"The method uses a two-stage recovery pipeline. A standard QAD stage first provides a stable low-bit initialization by distilling from a BF16 teacher on fixed corpus prefixes. OPD then switches to on-policy data: the student generates completions through the same quantized forward path used at deployment, and a frozen BF16 teacher provides token-level supervision on the prefixes produced by that student. Task verifiers add outcome-level signals: final-answer correctness for math and test execution for code. The important change is where supervision is applied. Training separate compu","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-22T12:44:16Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2609_26333.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"LLM 推理中的 prefill（计算密集型）和 decode（访存密集型）阶段对量化的需求不同，如何针对这两个阶段分别优化量化策略以兼顾精度和效率？","importance":"提出“解耦量化”（Disaggregated Quantization, DQ）范式，打破了 prefill 和 decode 必须使用相同量化格式和权重的惯例。这为 LLM 推理优化提供了一个新的设计空间，允许在不增加推理成本的情况下提升精度，或在保持精度的同时加速 prefill。","technical_approach":"引入解耦量化感知蒸馏（QADD），在训练中通过掩码区分 prefill 和 decode 路径。提出三种解耦方案：1) 格式解耦：prefill 用计算友好的量化（如 INT4/NVFP4），decode 用低比特仅权重压缩；2) 完全解耦：为 prefill 训练独立的计算原生权重，进一步提升低比特 decode 的精度；3) 卸载解耦 prefill (ODP)：将 prefill 权重放在 SSD，通过流式加载和计算重叠隐藏延迟，节省显存。","experiments":"在 Qwen 3 和 Gemma 3 上，格式解耦在 decode 密集型任务上提升了精度。完全解耦（训练独立的 NVFP4 prefill 权重）在 prefill 和 decode 密集型任务上，均能匹配或超越 2-3 bit 仅权重推理的精度，同时加速 prefill。结合 ODP，在 Qwen3.8-27B 上，8K 上下文时的首 token 时间比仅权重基线快 1.78 倍。为 1-bit GGUF 模型训练 NVFP4 prefiller，可将精度提升 32.5%。","reading_guide":"重点关注 Section 2 对三种解耦方案（格式解耦、完全解耦、ODP）的定义和取舍分析。Section 3 的实验结果展示了不同解耦策略在精度和速度上的具体收益，特别是 ODP 如何在长上下文中隐藏 I/O 延迟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The prefill (compute-bound) and decode (memory-bound) phases in LLM inference have different quantization needs. How can we optimize quantization strategies separately for these two phases to balance accuracy and efficiency?","importance":"Introduces the paradigm of 'Disaggregated Quantization' (DQ), breaking the convention that prefill and decode must use the same quantization formats and weights. This opens a new design space for LLM inference optimization, allowing for accuracy improvements without increasing inference cost, or prefill acceleration while maintaining accuracy.","technical_approach":"Introduces Quantization-Aware Distillation with Disaggregation (QADD), using masks during training to separate prefill and decode pathways. The root cause is floating-point non-associativity combined with hardware-dependent kernel selection. Inference frameworks select different matrix-multiplication kernels on each architecture, with different parallel reduction orders and unspecified tensor-core arithmetic, and the resulting rounding differ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-22T03:32:40Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2609_25624.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AR","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文处理的是 LLM 贪心解码在不同 GPU 架构上仍会产生不同输出的问题。根因不是随机采样，而是浮点非结合性、vendor GEMM 的架构相关 kernel 选择、tensor core 累加细节，以及 BF16/TF32 等低精度路径带来的舍入差异；当 top token 概率接近时，这些差异会改变 argmax。","importance":"这篇工作对预训练本身不是直接贡献，但对大模型工程和实验可复现性有实用启发。它把“跨 GPU 一致输出”从经验上的高概率一致，推进到 kernel 级可验证的 bitwise 一致。对基座研究员更相关的点在于：回归测试、长链路 agent replay、不同硬件上的评测复现、推理侧数值误差定位，都需要这种确定性基础。它也提醒训练和推理中的数值路径不能只看 FP32 权重，decode 阶段会增加显存带宽压力；同时 vendor GEMM 在不同 GPU 上仍可能选择不同 kernel，reduction order 不固定，所以只能降低 token 翻转概率，不能保证 bitwise 一致。新方案让 BF16 权重以 BF16 宽度从 HBM 读入，在寄存器中 upcast 到 FP32，再用 CUDA core 上的 IEEE-754 FMA 做 FP32 累加。为避免架构相关差异，方法禁用 tensor core/TF32 路径，kernel 配置由 problem shape 决定而不是 autotuning 决定，小 batch decode 使用确定性的 split-K，并按固定升序合并 partial sums，不用 atomic。这样每个 dot product 的浮点归约顺序成为输入 shape 的纯函数。","experiments":"论文在 Ampere、Ada、Hopper 三类 GPU 上验证线性层输出 bitwise identical，覆盖 A100、L40S、H100，测试包含 decode 和 prefill 形状、BF16 和 FP32 权重、以及 ragged dimensions。端到端接入 vLLM 后，相比已有 FP32-compute mitigation pipeline，吞吐提升约 1.17–3.1 倍，并最多节省 1.4 GiB 权重相关显存，可转化为更多 KV-cache 容量。一个有用的附带结果是 batch-invariant：在 decode bucket 内，同一请求的输出不受同 batch 中其他请求数量影响；这对 serving 系统的 run-to-run determinism 很关键。","reading_guide":"泛读建议 20–30 分钟。重点看 Introduction 中对现有 FP32-compute 方案的两个批评：带宽开销和 residual GEMM reduction nondeterminism。Design 部分值得细看，尤其是 fused-upcast、禁用 tensor core、固定 kernel 配置、deterministic split-K 这几处设计约束。实验部分主要看三类结果：跨架构 hash/bitwise 验证、vLLM 端到端吞吐、batch-invariance。若关心训练复现，可以把这篇作为推理侧数值确定性的参考，而不是直接照搬到训练 GEMM。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses nondeterministic LLM outputs under greedy decoding across GPU architectures. The issue is not sampling randomness; it comes from floating-point non-associativity, architecture-dependent vendor GEMM kernel selection, tensor-core accumulation details, and rounding differences in low-precision paths such as BF16 or TF32. When the top logits are close, these small differences can flip the argmax token.","importance":"This is not a pretraining-method paper, but it is relevant to reproducible model research and deployment. The contribution is a kernel-level path to bitwise cross-GPU reproducibility, rather than a statistical reduction in output divergence. For foundation-model work, the useful lesson is that regression testing, evaluation replay, agent debugging, and hardware comparisons depend on details below the model graph: dtype names are not enough; reduction order and kernel dispatch can affect observable model behavior.","technical_approach":"The method replaces the usual cast-then-GEMM pipeline with fixed-configuration fused-upcast Triton GEMMs. In the baseline mitigation, BF16 weights are materialized as temporary FP32 tensors and then passed to vendor GEMM. That reduces rounding error but makes decode bandwidth effectively FP32 and still leaves vendor-chosen reduction orders uncontrolled. Here, BF16 weights are loaded from HBM at BF16 width, upcast to FP32 in registers, and accumulated with IEEE-754 FMA on CUDA cores. The design removes sources of device-dependent variation: no tensor-core or TF32 path for FP32 accumulation, no autotuned per-device kernel choice, compile-time configurations selected by shape, and deterministic split-K for small-batch decode with partial sums combined in a fixed ascending order and without atomics. The reduction order is therefore determined by the problem shape.","experiments":"The paper reports bitwise-identical linear-layer outputs across Ampere, Ada, and Hopper GPUs, including A100, L40S, and H100. The probes cover decode and prefill regimes, BF16 and FP32 weights, and ragged dimensions. In an end-to-end vLLM integration, the system improves throughput by about 1.17–3.1x over the prior FP32-compute mitigation and saves up to 1.4 GiB of weight-related memory, which can be used for additional KV-cache capacity. It also demonstrates batch invariance within a decode bucket: a request’s outputs are bitwise independent of how many other requests are batched with it.","reading_guide":"Skim time: about 20–30 minutes. Read the Introduction for the diagnosis of why FP32-compute mitigation is still slow and only statistically reproducible. Then focus on the Design section: fused upcast, disabling tensor cores, fixed kernel configuration, and deterministic split-K are the core pieces. In the evaluation, look for three checks: cross-architecture bitwise validation, vLLM throughput, and batch-invariance. A long textual suffix may be unchanged, but its cached states are not necessarily valid because the causal prefix and RoPE positions may have changed. Reusing all suffix KV can propagate stale states, while recomputing the entire suffix wastes prefill work and offload bandwidth.","importance":"This is an inference-systems paper rather than a pretraining contribution. Its main relevance is in long-context deployment: KV reuse should not be defined only by token equality. The right question is whether a cached state is still valid, whether it can affect generation, and how precisely it must be restored. That framing is useful for agent runtimes, context compaction, evidence refresh in RAG, and debugging how local context edits propagate through long-context models.","technical_approach":"PatchKV decomposes adjacent context versions into an exact prefix, an updated span, and an aligned suffix. Instead of either reusing the whole suffix or recomputing everything after the first mismatch, it first identifies states that need semantic repair, then chooses an efficient representation for the rest. Offline, paired exact prefills are used to train a length-conditioned drift predictor for the edit-local dirty boundary. During checkpoint creation, the system stores compact attention statistics. At resume time, the predictor marks the local dirty region, attention-guided selection adds sparse nonlocal blocks that may matter for generation, and the block-aligned union becomes the repair set. Those blocks are recomputed under the revised context. Miscalibration causes problems in","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-22T14:28:31Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2609_26489.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文讨论的不是如何发明新的校准指标，而是为什么 LLM 评测体系长期没有把校准作为常规报告项。核心问题是：模型的正确率只能说明“答对多少”，不能说明“模型在答错时是否知道自己可能错”。在 LLM 被用于 agent、自动数据生成、LLM-as-a-judge 和主动学习时，这个缺口会直接影响系统可靠性和研究结论的可信度。","importance":"对预训练研究的启发主要在评测接口上。校准不是单纯的安全或部署指标，它会影响 pretrain 与 posttrain 的闭环判断：RLHF、指令微调、拒答策略、verbalized confidence 都可能改变模型概率与真实正确率之间的关系。如果只看 accuracy、F1、BLEU、ROUGE 或 benchmark 排名，很容易忽略模型是否在变得更会“表达确定性”，还是只是更会给出看似确定的答案。论文的贡献量级偏倡议和规范化，不是技术方法突破；价值在于把已有校准工具拉回主流 LLM evaluation。","technical_approach":"论文把 LLM 的置信度来源分成三类。第一类是自回归模型天然给出的 token 或 sequence probability，常用长度归一化来比较不同长度输出；问题是多选题里答案 token 的选取、长度偏置和选项表述都会影响置信度估计。第二类是 verbalized confidence，即让模型用自然语言或百分比表达“我有多确定”；对 instruction-tuned 模型，这个信号有时比原始概率更贴近用户实际看到的置信度。第三类是行为信号，例如犹豫、拒答、hedging、表达不确定。论文强调，标准校准度量通常只需要每个样本两个输入：一个 confidence score 和一个 correctness judgment。很多封闭式任务已经具备这两个输入；开放式生成的难点不在指标本身，而在如何定义可复现的正确性判断和置信度抽取方式。","experiments":"这不是一篇实验驱动论文，没有提出新模型或新 benchmark。主要证据来自文献整理和报告审查。作者检查了多个近期主流模型家族的公开技术报告和 model card，包括 OpenAI、Anthropic、Google DeepMind、DeepSeek、Meta、Qwen、Gemma、GPT-OSS、OLMo 等报告，指出这些报告通常覆盖大量能力和安全 benchmark，但没有系统报告校准指标。论文也提到 GPT-4 技术报告曾记录 RLHF 对校准的影响，是较早的例外。关键结论是：校准方法已经存在，采用不足才是主要瓶颈。","reading_guide":"泛读即可，预计 20–30 分钟。建议重点看 Introduction 中关于 adoption gap 的论证，以及 Calibration for LLMs 中对三类 confidence signal 的划分。对做 pretrain eval 的读者，最有用的是“两个输入”这个框架：confidence score 如何取、correctness judgment 如何定义。开放式生成部分如果全文有展开，可以重点看，因为它直接关系到长答案、代码、推理轨迹和 agent 输出的评测设计。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper is not trying to invent a new calibration metric. Its main point is that calibration is rarely treated as a standard reporting dimension in LLM evaluation. Accuracy tells us how often a model is correct, but not whether the model can signal when it is likely to be wrong. That missing signal matters for deployment, autonomous agents, LLM-as-a-judge pipelines, synthetic data generation, and active learning.","importance":"The relevance to pretraining research is mainly at the evaluation interface. Calibration is not only a deployment or safety property. It affects how we interpret pretraining, instruction tuning, RLHF, refusal behavior, and verbalized confidence. A model can improve on benchmark accuracy while becoming less reliable as a confidence source. The contribution is a methodological and reporting proposal rather than a new training method. Its value is in pushing existing calibration tools into routine LLM model reports and benchmark design.","technical_approach":"The paper separates LLM confidence into three signals. The first is token or sequence probability, the native confidence signal of an autoregressive model, often length-normalized for generated sequences. Even in multiple-choice QA, this requires design choices about answer tokens, option formatting, and length effects. The second is verbalized confidence, where the model states how sure it is; for instruction-tuned models this may better match the signal users actually consume. The third is behavioral confidence, including hedging, refusal, doubt, and other implicit cues. The central technical framing is simple: standard calibration metrics need a confidence score and a correctness judgment per example. Many existing benchmarks already contain enough structure for this. For open-ended generation, the main challenge is defining these two inputs consistently, not creating an entirely new family of metrics.","experiments":"This is not an experimental model paper. It does not introduce a new model, dataset, or calibration algorithm. Its evidence is based on prior calibration literature and a review of public technical reports and model cards from recent model families, including OpenAI, Anthropic, Google DeepMind, DeepSeek, Meta, Qwen, Gemma, GPT-OSS, and OLMo. The paper argues that these reports typically include many capability and safety benchmarks but do not report calibration. The GPT-4 technical report is noted as an earlier exception because it documented the effect of RLHF on calibration.","reading_guide":"A skim read is enough, roughly 20–30 minutes. We study how local serving stacks affect this protocol step and show that measured outcomes can depend on the serving layer rather than model behavior alone. a","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-22T16:48:17Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2609_26693.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI","cs.SE"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文要解决的不是工具调用能力本身，而是工具调用评测里的一个隐藏测量误差：本地 agent 评测通常把“模型是否会正确选择并发出工具调用”当成模型能力，但在实际栈里，请求先经过 serving layer，是否接受 tools= 参数、是否把 schema 渲染进 prompt、是否把输出解析成结构化 tool_call，往往由服务端模板和配置决定，而不是由模型权重决定。结果是，评测可能测到的是 Ollama、llama.cpp、vLLM、SGLang 这些 serving stack 的协议行为，而不是模型的 tool-use 能力。","importance":"对基座研究的直接相关性不在 pretrain 方法，而在 evaluation hygiene。现在很多小模型、本地模型、代码 agent 的结论都建立在 OpenAI-compatible serving 接口之上；如果 serving 层在推理前就拒绝请求，或者把错误吞成普通 assistant turn，下游统计会把“模型根本没运行”误记成“模型不会调用工具”。这会污染模型比较、家族比较、尺度比较，也会误导后续数据合成和 post-training 方向。对做 LLM 训练的人，这篇文章的价值在于提醒：协议层、模板层、解析层都可能成为隐变量，评测设计必须把模型行为和系统行为拆开记录，否则结论不稳。","technical_approach":"方法上它是一篇 measurement study，不提出新的 agent 架构。作者固定一个 ReAct 风格 coding-agent harness，在需要多次工具调用的聚合任务上，专门测“协议保真度”而不是任务成功率。核心做法有三点。\n\n第一，显式区分 channel 与 prompt。所谓 channel，是工具 schema 通过原生 tools= 字段传给服务端，再由服务端模板渲染并解析；prompt 则是把工具列表和 JSON 调用格式直接写进文本提示。作者设计了三种设置：native，保持默认 tools=；native+hint，仍走原生 channel，但额外在 prompt 中加入文本工具列表和严格 JSON 格式提示；text-tools，完全去掉 tools=，只靠文本提示并从文本中解析调用。这样可以把“服务端是否接受原生工具调用协议”和“模型是否理解工具说明”分开。\n\n第二，重新定义并细分评测标签。每个 assistant turn 被标成 valid in-schema call、hallucinated call、unparseable text、no-call prose、non-response。特别关键的是 non-response：在 Ollama 某些模型上，HTTP 400 的 request rejection 会触发 harness 重试，最终把 generic error string 写入轨迹；如果不保留结构化错误元数据，下游会把它误判成模型没发起工具调用。作者因此把 non-response 从 fidelity 分母中剥离，单独报告，避免 0% fidelity 这种假阴性。\n\n第三，做多 serving stack 对照。相同权重、相同请求，在 Ollama、llama.cpp、vLLM、SGLang 上的行为不同：有的直接拒绝 tools=，有的接受但只返回文本形式调用，有的需要显式 parser 配置才认 tool_call。这一设计的目的不是比较谁更好，而是证明“结果依赖 serving stack”。文中还加入 constrained decoding baseline，说明即便强制输出合法调用格式，也可能把失败模式从‘不发 call’转成‘循环不终止’，并没有真正解决 agent 评测问题。","experiments":"实验覆盖 Qwen2.5-Coder 0.5B/1.5B/3B、Llama-3.2、Phi-3、Gemma-3 等小模型，并在 8 个 seed 上报告 turn-pooled rate 与 per-seed mean，使用 seed-level bootstrap 95% CI。最重要的发现有四个。第一，在 Ollama 默认 native function calling 路径下，Qwen2.5-Coder 系列能接受 tools= 但把调用作为文本返回，Llama-3.2 返回原生 tool_calls，而 Phi-3 和 Gemma-3 会在推理前被 HTTP 400 拒绝；如果 harness 不保留拒绝元数据，这两类模型会被错误记成 0% fidelity。第二，对服务端接受 tools= 的模型，native+hint 往往能显著恢复或提升协议保真度，说明很多失败并不来自模型不会调用工具，而是原生 channel 没把约束表达清楚；但对 Llama-3.2，统一的 text protocol 反而降低表现，说明最佳配置是模型相关的。第三，同一权重跨四个 serving stack 的结果不一致：Ollama 拒绝的模型可在 llama.cpp 上运行，vLLM 默认也会拒绝同类请求，SGLang 则接受但把调用作为文本返回。这直接证明测量对象混入了 serving 层。第四，作者指出 pooled 与 per-seed 指标可相差约 55 个点，因为单个长循环 episode 会主导 pooled 统计；这说明 agent 评测在薄样本下对聚合方式非常敏感。\n\n论文还有一个诚实的负结果：在这种测量精度下，作者无法支持关于模型规模、家族、是否 reasoning model 更擅长工具调用等更大结论，因此明确拒绝做这些推断。这一点比很多 benchmark 论文更稳。","reading_guide":"泛读建议很明确。先看引言和方法部分关于 OpenAI-compatible tool calling 的协议链路：tools= 并不是直接喂给模型，而是先经过服务端模板渲染和输出解析，这一层如果没想清楚，后面的结果都容易误读。然后看实验里三种设置 native / native+hint / text-tools 的对照表，以及四个 serving stack 的行为差异图，这两处最能说明问题。最后看作者如何定义 non-response 并把它从 fidelity 分母中剥离，这个细节决定了 0% 结果是否可信。\n\n如果时间有限，20 分钟足够抓住主旨。若要用于自己搭评测栈，建议顺手抄下文中的 checklist：记录 HTTP/serving 错误、区分请求未执行与模型未调用、同时报告 pooled 和 per-seed、明确 parser/template 配置。它不是一篇方法论文，但对任何做本地 agent benchmark、tool-use SFT 数据构造、或小模型部署评测的人，都能省掉不少无效结论。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper is not about improving tool-use itself. It studies a hidden measurement failure in local tool-use evaluation: benchmarks often treat “did the model emit a valid tool call?” as a model capability, but in practice the request first passes through a serving layer. Whether tools= is accepted, whether schemas are rendered into the prompt, and whether outputs are parsed back into structured tool_calls can all be determined by server templates and launch configuration rather than by the model weights. It is relevant to post-training pipelines because it isolates a silent failure mode: trains but does not learn. In this failure, loss decreases and monitoring signals remain green, yet the delivered model does not improve on the customer’s intended task. This matters for SFT, RL, continual pretraining, and managed model delivery, where the reliability of training signals, held-out acceptance data, model cards, cost reconciliation, and registry checks determines whether a trained artifact can be trusted.","technical_approach":"The benchmark creates a governed delivery plane with ten stages: intake, plan, config, schedule, train, eval, register, deploy, cost review, and handoff. A TBDL run passes the training gate but fails an operator-run acceptance gate. A TBDL delivery occurs when the agent ships such a run as the final product.\n\nThe design separates failures that deterministic tooling can catch from failures that require delivery-level judgment. Config, scheduling, and cost review are certified by scripted references before agent evaluation, making these failures cheap and visible. Intake, planning, evaluation, and deployment failures require the delivery oracle. Training, registration, and handoff are constrained by the delivery contract. We observe the same problem in video, where this framework has not yet been explored. We therefore build Video-HopChain, a dataset of 22,550 mul","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-22T07:08:13Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2609_25773.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是视频推理 RLVR 训练里的两个具体缺口。第一，现有可验证视频问答数据虽然容易打分，但很多题目并不要求模型沿着时间轴持续取证，模型可以靠语言先验、局部线索，甚至不认真看视频也拿到奖励，因此训练信号对“真正的视频多步推理”约束不够。第二，GRPO 这类组相对优势方法在视频任务上同样会遇到 zero-variance group：同一题的一组采样如果全对或全错，优势为零，这一组样本几乎不给更新信号，训练效率被直接吃掉。","importance":"对基座研究的意义主要不在视频 QA 本身，而在两点。其一，它把 HopChain 这类“可验证多跳推理数据合成”从静态图像推进到视频，说明 RLVR 数据设计的关键不是答案是否可验证，而是问题是否迫使模型跨时间、多次回看证据。这个判断对多模态预训练和后训练都成立。其二，它给了一个很轻量的 GRPO 采样修补思路：不增加 rollout 数量，而是在高置信 token 位置做受控探索，专门打破组内零方差。这个思路对任何依赖组内相对奖励的训练都可能有参考价值，尤其是在 reward 稀疏、样本昂贵的视频场景。","technical_approach":"方法分成数据和训练两部分。数据侧，Video-HopChain 的核心设计不是简单把单跳视频问答串起来，而是把一个问题写成 3 到 6 个依赖 hop 的链。每个 hop 都是关于一个或两个视频时刻的 yes/no 判断，并映射到两个整数之一；最终答案是所有 hop 对应整数的求和，因此可以 exact match 验证。这里有两个关键约束。第一，题目里至少包含一个“时序关系” hop，需要比较两个时刻的先后或对应关系，避免单帧证据就能解题。第二，部分 hop 是 selector：前一步答案会决定后一步该去看哪个时刻，这样链式依赖更强，模型不能把各步独立做完再拼接。生成流程上，作者先用 VLM 做 shot-level caption，再让纯文本 LLM 基于这些镜头描述写出多跳题。这种两阶段生成的取舍很明确：直接让 VLM 端到端出复杂题更容易漂移，也更难保证可验证性；先把视频离散成镜头描述，再在文本空间构造逻辑链，更容易控制题型和答案结构。\n\n训练侧，作者在 GRPO 上提出 Confidence-Gated Exploration。标准 GRPO 依赖同题 G 个 rollout 的 reward 方差来形成相对优势；如果组内奖励完全一致，就没有学习信号。CGE 的做法是，当 G=8 时先正常采样前 4 条；若这 4 条全对或全错，说明这一题当前很可能会形成零方差组，于是后 4 条在 reasoning span 内启用 top-token mask：凡是某位置上模型对单一 token 的概率超过阈值 τ=0.95，就临时屏蔽这个 token，并在剩余 token 上重归一化采样。这样做不是盲目加噪声，而是只在“模型过于自信、且这组样本大概率无梯度”的位置强制探索。与此同时，被 mask 的位置不计入 token-level loss，但这 8 条 rollout 仍共同参与 group advantage 计算。这样既避免了重新补采样带来的额外 rollout 成本，也避免把明显被干预的 token 直接当作监督目标去拟合。","experiments":"数据规模上，训练集包含 22,550 个多跳问题，覆盖 13,378 个视频；另有 1,000 个 held-out 视频，每个视频 1 个问题，构成 benchmark。训练对象是 Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct，使用 GRPO 继续训练，并在 8 个通用视频 benchmark 上评估。基线模型的 8 项平均分为 55.4；加入 Video-HopChain 后升到 57.9，提升 2.5 分，而且 8 个 benchmark 全部上涨。这一点比单一榜单涨分更重要，说明这种专门为多跳取证设计的数据没有明显过拟合到自家题型。再叠加 CGE 后，平均分进一步到 59.3，相比仅用 Video-HopChain 再增 1.4 分，相比原始基线总增幅 3.9 分。\n\n从文中叙述看，作者还专门分析了 GRPO 的 advantage collapse，并把 CGE 的收益归因于“恢复原本无信号的组”，而不是简单增加采样随机性。值得注意的是，这篇工作的实验重点是泛化到通用视频理解 benchmark，而不是只在自建 benchmark 上刷分；这使它更像一篇关于训练信号设计的工作，而不只是数据集论文。局限也比较清楚：数据仍由 caption 驱动生成，最终上限受 shot caption 质量约束；另外奖励仍是最终答案级别的 exact match，没有对中间 hop 正误做更细粒度信用分配。","reading_guide":"泛读时建议先看 Introduction 里对视频 RLVR 数据问题的界定，再看数据构造部分，重点抓住三个设计：多跳链、时序比较、selector hop。只看这三点，就能明白它为什么比普通可验证视频 QA 更能逼模型看视频。然后看 CGE A model can often rely on priors, shallow cues, or partial observations and still get reward, so the training signal does not adequately supervise genuine multi-step video reasoning. Second, GRPO-style group-relative optimization suffers from zero-variance groups in this setting as well: if all rollouts for a question are either correct or incorrect, the group contributes almost no learning signal.","importance":"Its value is less about video QA as an application and more about training signal design. measure","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-22T07:36:04Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2609_25804.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的是：长程 agent 任务里，模型在中途做出的方向性决策好坏，现有 benchmark 几乎测不到。传统评测只看最终是否完成任务，但很多失败并不是执行能力差，而是在某个分叉点选错了方向，例如选错实验假设、实现路线或调试策略。作者把这种“在看不到最终结果时，仍能选出更有前景方向”的能力定义为 taste，并进一步提出一个关键问题：能否不依赖昂贵专家标注，而是直接从已有 agent 轨迹中自动挖出这种长程判断能力的监督信号与评测样本。","importance":"对预训练本身不是直接贡献，但对 post-train、agent training、以及长期决策数据构造有现实意义。它的价值不在于提出了新的 agent scaffold，而在于把一个过去被 end-to-end success 掩盖的能力单独测量出来。对基座研究员而言，启发主要有三点。第一，很多 agent 能力瓶颈可能不是 token-level execution，而是 trajectory-level judgment，这意味着训练数据的监督粒度需要变化。第二，作者展示了如何从多次尝试的轨迹里自动挖出 preference-like supervision，这和从 rollout 中构造偏好数据、过程监督、甚至 RL credit assignment 有明显关联。第三，taste 随决策时间跨度增加而变难，这给长程推理和 agent benchmark 设计提供了更细的难度轴。量级上，这是一个有方法味道的评测工作，适合关注 agent/post-train 接口的人看。","technical_approach":"方法核心是把长程决策评测转成 decision fork 上的二选一判断。作者观察到，在软件工程和机器学习研究任务中，agent 往往会对同一任务做多次尝试，或者在单次运行中先走弯路再自我修正。只要两条轨迹在某个时刻之前前缀等价、之后分叉，并且后续结果能清楚表明哪条方向更好，就可以把这个分叉点构造成一个 taste question。每个问题包含任务描述、分叉前的轨迹前缀、以及两个候选方向；模型需要在看不到后续执行结果的情况下，选出更优方向。作者提出两种挖掘方式：parallel trajectories，从同一任务的独立尝试中找分叉；detour trajectories，从单次运行中 agent 自己纠偏的过程里找“先错后改”的分叉。为了减少标签噪声，作者并不是简单用最终成败打标签，而是筛选那些“结果差异主要由分叉处判断导致”的样本，并用后续证据如测试结果、研究分数等定义 outcome。除此之外，论文还做了一个训练设定：让 teacher 在知道正确方向的条件下生成 reasoning，再把这种 reasoning distill 给 student，用来提升未见任务上的 taste judgment。这个设计很像把 hindsight supervision 转成可泛化的判断策略，而不是只记住 benchmark。","experiments":"作者构建并发布了 Taste-Bench，共 502 个 taste questions，覆盖软件工程和机器学习研究两类长程任务。实验首先验证 benchmark 本身：随着 decision fork 的时间跨度增加，题目难度上升，说明它确实在测长程判断而不是局部模式匹配。其次，作者做了人工复核，发现当评审者能看到后续结果时，人工判断与自动挖掘标签高度一致，支持标签质量。模型评测部分表明，现有模型在 taste 上仍有明显不足，而且 end-to-end 强并不必然意味着 taste 强。更重要的是，taste 是可训练的：用 teacher 在正确方向条件下生成的 reasoning 进行蒸馏后，模型在未见任务上的 fork judgment 提升，并且这种提升能转移到 held-out agent 任务的端到端表现。给出的全文片段没有完整数值表，因此不宜编造具体准确率或增幅；但从论文叙述看，作者不仅做了 benchmark 排名，还验证了“taste 训练 -> 真实 agent 任务收益”这条链路，这比单纯做一个评测集更完整。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读偏精读之间。先看 Introduction，把 taste 与普通成功率评测区分清楚。然后重点读 Section 3 左右关于 fork 构造的部分，尤其是 parallel fork 和 detour fork 的定义、筛选条件、标签来源，这决定了 benchmark 是否可信。接着看人工验证和 horizon 难度分析，判断它是不是在测真正的长程决策。若你关心训练信号设计，再看 distillation 部分：teacher 看见正确方向后生成 reasoning，这种 hindsight-to-policy 的转化很值得琢磨。实验部分优先看 benchmark 统计、不同模型在不同 horizon 上的表现、以及蒸馏前后在 held-out tasks 上的变化。预计 45 到 60 分钟能读完关键内容。值得深挖的问题有两个：一是 fork 标签是否仍然混入执行质量差异；二是这种 hindsight supervision 能否推广到更通用的 agent RL 或 process reward 建模。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a missing capability in current long-horizon agent evaluation: the quality of intermediate strategic decisions. Existing benchmarks mostly score end-to-end success, but many failures come from choosing the wrong direction at a fork point rather than from poor local execution. Examples include selecting the wrong hypothesis, implementation path, or debugging strategy. The authors call the ability to choose the better direction under uncertainty an agent’s taste. judge；如果 judge 成本太高，评测覆盖面和数据过滤规模会受限。这篇工作的启发是：judge 不一定都要生成长推理。对于偏好比较、证据约束的事实性判断等常规样本，decision-only judge 可以承担便宜的第一遍过滤；但涉及推导核查、数学/代码正确性、以及文风掩盖错误答案的样本，仍然需要更强模型的推理能力。贡献量级偏工程经验和评测方法，不是新的训练目标或模型架构。","technical_approach":"论文评估 TypeSafe JEV 这类 hosted decision-only judge。输入仍然是自然语言 rubric rationale，以控制输出格式差异。实验重点放在三件事：准确率、概率质量、费用/延迟。路由策略采用 frozen cascade：在选择集上拟合温度和置信阈值，高置信样本接受 JEV 判定，低置信样本升级到更强 LLM judge。这个设计的关键点是局部验证，因为论文发现阈值跨任务迁移并不稳定，尤其是 reference-free prose pilot 在推理前冻结，并按源问题切成 40/60 的选择集和测试集；670 个 extension 在查看 pilot 准确率前冻结。结果显示，JEV 在普通偏好和 evidence-grounded factuality 上距离最强 LLM comparator 约 3 个百分点以内，费用约为 comparator 的 0.36%。较大差距集中在需要检查推导、数学/代码正确性，或需要抵抗“写得很完整但实际错误”的回答时。置信度分析显示，在若干 benchmark 上，JEV 与强 judge 的差距主要集中于低置信决策，因此 cascade 能用较低成本保留相当一部分强 judge 质量。但作者也指出，固定阈值不能无条件迁移到新任务。","reading_guide":"泛读建议约 25–35 分钟。先看 的准确率/费用对比，置信度分桶后的错误集中情况，以及 cascade 在不同阈值下的 cost-quality 曲线。附录里的 rubric 和 split 细节适合在复现实验或设计内部 judge pipeline 时参考。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks whether a cheap decision-only judge can serve as the first pass in large-scale LLM evaluation, accepting easy cases and escalating uncertain ones to a stronger generative judge. The central issue is not the typed output interface itself, but whether the exposed label probabilities are calibrated enough to support routing.","importance":"The main relevance is evaluation infrastructure for pretraining and post-training pipelines. Automatic judges are now used for model comparison, data filtering, preference construction, and regression testing. If every decision requires a strong reasoning model, evaluation cost becomes a limiting factor. The paper gives a practical answer: decision-only judging is useful for routine preference and evidence-grounded factuality, but it should not replace reasoning-heavy judging for derivations, math, code, or polished wrong answers. The contribution is mostly empirical and systems-facing rather than a new modeling method.","technical_approach":"The study evaluates TypeSafe JEV, a hosted judge that takes natural-language instructions plus structured inputs and returns a label distribution over a specified output type. The judge output is separated from the answer format: free-form answers can be judged through a label-only interface. The comparison includes sixteen generative and reward-model judges. Generative baselines are also constrained to a decision-and-probability contract without rationales, which makes the comparison focus on complete judge configurations rather than explanation length. The selective evaluation policy is a frozen cascade: temperatures and confidence thresholds are fitted on a selection split, confident JEV decisions are accepted, and low-confidence cases are routed to a stronger LLM judge. A key methodological point is that threshold transfer is treated as empirical; the paper does not assume that one confidence cutoff works across tasks.","experiments":"$26","reading_guide":"Skim time: about 25–35 minutes. This matters if agents are used to maintain model support, decoding features, distributed execution paths, and public serving APIs.","technical_approach":"The benchmark contains 53 repository-grounded tasks derived from recent merged changes in SGLang, spanning six families of inference engineering. Candidate patches are scored by behavioral tests: fail-to-pass tests for required new behavior, pass-to-pass tests for regression protection, plus end-to-end serving tests or calibrated performance gates when needed. Task validity is checked with no-op and oracle controls, agent-assisted adversarial probing, and human adjudication. Evaluation integrity is protected with closed-book execution and trajectory auditing.","experiments":"The central empirical finding is a production-correctness gap. In the Gemma 4 MoE core-serving task, 16 out of 33 agent-created patches, or 48.5%, passed every non-E2E test but failed to serve the specified model correctly through the live server interface. Removing model-serving E2E tests from scoring, while keeping the same patches and all other tests, increases pass rate by 23.4 percentage points, with gains in all 11 top-per-model configurations. We argue that compile rate is a scientifically unreliable metric for single-function vulnerability repair, and we support this with five controlled experiments over 203 vulnerable functions from Big-Vul, three open-source code LLMs (350M to 6.7B parameters), and three prompting strategies","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-22T17:32:05Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2609_26749.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.SE","cs.AI","cs.CR"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的不是“如何修漏洞”，而是更基础的“我们到底在用什么指标衡量修漏洞”。作者聚焦单函数级 C/C++ 漏洞修复这一常见设定，系统检验 compile rate 这个社区里最常见的自动指标是否真的能反映修复质量。全文给出的结论很明确：在 isolated-function 的评测流程里，compile rate 既不敏感，也不干净，还容易被评测脚手架和数据预处理伪影主导，因此不能作为科学上可靠的进展指标。进一步地，作者还检查了常见的相似度替代指标 CodeBLEU，发现 whole-function CodeBLEU 会把大量未改动上下文当成“高质量修复”的证据，甚至原样复制漏洞函数都能拿到比模型输出更高的分数。","importance":"对预训练研究本身，这篇论文不是方法创新，而是评测卫生学上的纠偏。它的价值在于提醒代码模型研究里一个常见问题：如果指标和真实目标脱钩，那么模型、数据、prompt、甚至 scaling 的比较都会被污染。对做 code pretraining 或 code LM 的人，这个结论很实际：单函数 compile rate 不能稳定区分模型能力，也不能可靠反映训练改进是否真的带来更好的修复行为。更重要的是，作者把误差来源拆开了，指出约 64% 的 compile failure 不是模型写错代码，而是来自缺失项目上下文和 Big-Vul 预处理伪影，而且这个比例在 350M 到 6.7B 三个模型上几乎不变。这意味着很多看似“模型差异”的结果，实际是在测 harness 和 dataset artifact。对任何依赖 proxy metric 的 LLM 研究，这都是一个可迁移的警示。","technical_approach":"方法上，这篇论文的强项是控制变量做得比较扎实。作者没有停留在“compile rate 不够好”的泛泛批评，而是设计了五类受控实验，分别对应一个指标失效模式。第一类看指标对真实改进是否敏感：通过能明显改善生成代码的干预，检查 compile rate 是否随之提升。第二类看混杂因素：把模型输出错误与评测设置错误分离，分析 isolated-function compile harness、stub injection、-fsyntax-only 编译方式以及 Big-Vul 预处理带来的失败。第三类看 toolchain 依赖：同一补丁在不同编译配置下的结果是否稳定。第四类看与独立指标的一致性：compile rate 与其他质量信号是否同向。第五类看可被优化利用的程度：如果直接朝 compile 优化，模型是否会学到删除代码、保守复制、规避语法错误这类“过指标不过任务”的行为。\n\n在替代指标部分，作者重点分析了 whole-function CodeBLEU 的结构性偏差。因为真实修复通常只改很小一段，函数的大部分 token 都保持不变，所以只要保留上下文，哪怕完全不修漏洞，也能拿到很高的分数。为此作者引入 diff_F1 作为 change-aware screen，而不是最终质量指标。它只在编辑区域上做重叠比较，因此 no-op copy 会得到 0 分，一些为了编译通过而做的大幅删除也只能拿到接近 0 的分数。这个设计的出发点不是“编辑越像开发者越好”，而是先把最明显的伪好结果筛掉。作者也比较克制地说明了它的边界：对某些 deletion-style patch，diff_F1 仍可能给出偏高信用，所以它只能作为 screen，不能替代测试用例或 exploit oracle。","experiments":"实验规模在这一子方向里算比较扎实：203 个 Big-Vul 漏洞函数，覆盖 7 类 CWE；3 个开源代码模型，参数规模从 350M 到 6.7B；3 种 prompting 策略；每种设置采样 5 次，并给出 bootstrap confidence interval。关键结果有几条。\n\n第一，compile rate 对真正改善代码的干预反应很弱，说明它缺乏区分力。摘要里没有给出这一项的具体绝对提升数字，但全文的论证重点是：即便生成质量有可观察改善，compile rate 也未必同步变化。\n\n第二，约 64% 的 compile failure 不能归因于模型本身，而是来自评测流程和数据问题，这个比例在不同模型规模上几乎不变。这个结果很关键，因为它直接说明 compile rate 的大头方差不是 model quality。\n\n第三，作者用受控分析表明 whole-function CodeBLEU 明显失真：直接复制原始漏洞函数的 no-op baseline，得分高于所有被测模型。这几乎把这个指标在单函数修复场景里的解释性直接打掉了。\n\n第四，diff_F1 在作者观察到的几类 gaming patch 上更稳：对 no-op 给 0 分，对某些通过删代码换取 compile 的补丁给接近 0 的分数，同时对与开发者编辑区域有重叠的部分修复给出非零信用。不过作者也明确承认它并不总能压低所有 deletion patch 的分数。\n\n第五，论文还给出一个相对少见的 benchmark-scale repair comparison。虽然主旨不是比谁修得更好，但 203-function 规模、三模型三策略的对比，比很多只做几十个样本的工作更能说明指标噪声和置信区间问题。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，但建议把方法和结果部分看细一点。优先看 Introduction 末尾列出的五个 failure modes，先建立作者到底在批评什么。然后直接看 Section 3 里 dataset artifact、isolated-function compile harness、metrics 定义，这几处决定了后面所有结论是否站得住。Section 4 是核心，重点看：\n1. compile failure 的归因拆分，尤其是 64% 非模型原因这一段；\n2. whole-function CodeBLEU 为什么会让 no-op 胜出；\n3. diff_F1 的定义、例子和失败案例。\n\n如果时间有限，先看 Figure 1 的评测流程图，再看 Section 4.3、4.5、4.7。预计 25 到 35 分钟能抓住主结论。值得深挖的点有两个：一是 isolated-function 评测在 code LM 里到底还能不能用，二是 change-aware metric 能否进一步结合静态分析或 exploit-aware oracle，形成比 diff_F1 更可靠的自动筛查链路。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper is not really about building a better vulnerability repair model. It asks a more basic question: what are we actually measuring when we claim progress in LLM-based single-function vulnerability repair? The target is compile rate, a widely used automatic proxy in C/C++ repair benchmarks. After reading the full paper, the conclusion is quite direct: under isolated-function evaluation, compile rate is not a scientifically reliable measure of repair quality. It is weakly responsive to genuine improvements, heavily confounded by harness and dataset artifacts, sensitive to toolchain details, and easy to game. The paper also examines whole-function CodeBLEU as a fallback metric and shows that it mostly rewards preserved context rather than repair; a no-op copy of the vulnerable function can outscore all tested models.","importance":"For pretraining researchers, this is not a new training method but an evaluation correction with practical consequences. If the proxy metric is misaligned with the real objective, then comparisons across models, prompts, data mixtures, or scaling trends become hard to trust. That matters for code LMs because many papers still rely on cheap automatic metrics when executable tests are unavailable. The most useful contribution here is the decomposition of failure sources: about 64% of compile failures are attributed not to model-generated bad code, but to missing project context and a Big-Vul preprocessing artifact, and this fraction stays nearly constant across models from 350M to 6.7B. That means a large part of the measured variance is not model quality at all. The lesson generalizes beyond code repair: any LLM setting built on weak proxies should separate model error from evaluation-pipeline error before drawing capability conclusions.","technical_approach":"$27","experiments":"$28","reading_guide":"This is a skim paper, but it is worth reading the methodology and results more carefully than the title might suggest. Where does this encoding happen when such representations are not provided? We find that the transformer can internalize this missing computation, constructing task-usable perceptual representations within its o","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-22T14:41:33Z","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2609_26513.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文研究一个很具体但重要的问题：当多模态Transformer不再依赖独立视觉/音频编码器，而是直接接收轻投影的patch、frame或离散视觉token时，原本由感知编码器完成的“表征整理”到底发生在哪里。作者的回答是：共享Transformer内部会自发形成一个功能带，通常位于早期到中期层，先把原始感知token组织成可供下游语言模块读取的表征；这个功能带虽然没有显式模块边界，但在计算上扮演了“虚拟编码器”的角色。","importance":"对预训练研究的价值主要有三层。第一，它给encoder-free和discrete-token多模态模型提供了一个更清晰的内部计算图景，说明“架构统一”不等于“计算完全混合”，共享Transformer内部仍可能出现阶段化分工。第二，这对多模态预训练设计有直接启发：如果早层承担感知编码职责，那么层深分配、位置编码、模态token注入方式、训练损失落点，都可能影响虚拟编码器是否形成以及形成得是否稳定。第三，它也影响模型解释与蒸馏思路：如果虚拟编码器是稳定存在的，就可以考虑显式监督、层级路由、早层专门化甚至轻量外接编码器替代。贡献量级上，这是一篇机制分析论文，不提出新的预训练算法，但对native multimodal transformer的设计判断有实质帮助。","technical_approach":"论文把现有MLLM分成三类：encoder-full、encoder-free、discrete-token。encoder-full先用独立感知编码器把图像或音频变成连续语义特征，再送入共享Transformer；后两类则把感知输入更直接地送进共享Transformer，因此作者提出假设：缺失的编码计算会被Transformer前部内化。\n\n为验证这个假设，作者主要用了三类分析。第一是layerwise linear probing，检查不同层的模态token隐藏状态何时开始线性可分地承载概念语义。如果是encoder-full，输入一开始就应已有较成熟表征；如果是encoder-free或discrete-token，则应随层深逐步形成。第二是representational similarity analysis，把中间层表示与专用感知编码器的表示几何进行比较，看早中层是否呈现encoder-like geometry。第三是causal intervention，通过对图像和音频输入做扰动，并观察不同层模态token对最终输出的因果必要性，定位“表征形成并被读出”的深度。\n\n主分析对象是Gemma 4 12B，同时做跨模型比较：encoder-free包括EVE和Fuyu，discrete-token包括Chameleon，encoder-full对照包括LLaVA-1.5、Qwen3-VL、Qwen3-Omni、Qwen2-Audio。数据上作者构造了一个图像-音频配对概念集，基于ImageNet-1k与ESC-50的31个共享概念，每个概念40张图和40段音频，总计每模态1240样本，用于跨模态层级分析。\n\n方法上的关键判断不是“某层最强”，而是“是否存在一个连续层段承担了从低层感知结构到可读语义表征的组织过程”。作者把这个层段定义为Virtual Encoder，并进一步区分不同模态在读出后的路由轨迹：Gemma 4中视觉表征在接近读出处会短暂向语言子空间对齐，而音频轨迹明显不同，说明共享Transformer内部并非简单地把所有模态压成同一种中间语义流。","experiments":"核心结果有四类。第一，线性探测显示encoder-free和discrete-token模型的概念可解码性会随层深逐步上升，而encoder-full模型在进入共享Transformer前已经拥有较成熟的感知表征，因此早层就能读出较强语义。这支持“编码过程被内化到共享Transformer前部”的假设。第二，表示相似性分析表明，Gemma 4 12B等模型的早中层隐藏状态在几何上接近专用感知编码器，说明这些层不是在做纯语言处理，而是在组织感知结构。第三，因果分析定位了表征被下游读取的深度：在某一层段之前，扰动模态token会显著影响输出；超过该层段后，影响方式发生变化，说明编码后的表征已被后续模块接管。第四，模态路由并不统一：Gemma 4中视觉在读出附近会短暂靠近语言子空间，音频则沿不同轨迹传播，提示视觉和音频在共享Transformer中的融合机制并不对称。\n\n从可见内容看，论文更强调趋势和层级结构，而不是追求大规模benchmark分数。数据规模也较小：31个共享概念、每模态1240样本，因此结论应理解为机制证据而非覆盖所有开放域语义的统计定律。它的说服力主要来自多种分析手段相互印证，以及跨架构对照的清晰性，而不是单一指标上的大幅数值提升。","reading_guide":"这篇适合认真泛读。先看Introduction和Figure 1/2，弄清作者区分的是“架构边界”与“功能边界”。随后重点读The Virtual Encoder部分，尤其是三个研究问题：语义何时形成、几何是否像编码器、因果上何时被读出。这三问基本就是全文主线。实验部分建议按方法读，而不是按模型读：先看linear probing结果，再看representation similarity，最后看causal analysis，因为三者组合起来才构成“虚拟编码器存在”的证据链。若只看一项，很容易把现象误读成普通层级表征演化。时间预算大约45-60分钟。若要深挖，建议额外思考两个问题：一是虚拟编码器是否会随训练阶段或数据配比移动层位；二是这种早层专门化是否意味着可以在预训练时加入显式辅助损失或层间模块化设计。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a precise but important question: when a multimodal transformer does not rely on a dedicated vision or audio encoder, and instead consumes lightly projected patches, frames, or discrete perceptual tokens directly, where does the missing perceptual encoding happen? The answer is that the shared transformer develops an internal functional regime, typically in early-to-middle layers, that organizes raw perceptual tokens into task-usable representations before the downstream language processing stages. The authors call this regime a Virtual Encoder.","importance":"The main value for pretraining research is conceptual clarity about computation inside native multimodal transformers. It shows that architectural unification does not imply fully homogeneous computation. Even in a single shared transformer, perception and language can remain functionally staged. This matters for multimodal pretraining design: if early layers are effectively acting as perceptual encoders, then depth allocation, token injection strategy, positional treatment, and where losses are applied may all affect whether this regime forms reliably. It also matters for interpretability and distillation. If the virtual encoder is stable, one can imagine explicit supervision, modular routing, or lightweight external replacements. This is an analysis paper rather than a new training recipe, but it gives useful guidance for designing encoder-free and discrete-token multimodal models.","technical_approach":"$2d","experiments":"$2e","reading_guide":"This is worth a careful skim. Start with the introduction and the main figures to understand the distinction between architectural boundaries and functional boundaries. Then focus on the Virtual Encoder section, especially the three core questions: when semantics emerges, whether intermediate geometry looks encoder-like, and at what depth the representation is causally read out. In the experiments, read by analysis type rather than by model: linear probing first, then representational similarity, then causal intervention. The argument is cumulative, and any single analysis on its own is easier to misread as ordinary layerwise representation evolution. A 45 to 60 minute pass is enough to capture the main contribution.
